SELECT p."Id" AS id, p."Name" AS name, p."SKU" AS sku, CASE
    WHEN p."SKU" IS NOT NULL THEN 'BARCODE-' || p."SKU"
    ELSE ''
END AS barcode, p."IsActive" AS "isActive"
